16	
17	# LaTeX 论文写作全流程
18	
19	ML/AI 顶会论文从草稿到投稿的完整指南。整合 Orchestra Research、VibeSkills、claim2evidence 三方最佳实践。
20	
21	---
22	
23	## 1. 工作模式
24	
25	开始前选择模式：
26	
27	| 模式 | 说明 | 适用 |
28	|------|------|------|
29	| **Build** | 从实验笔记/结果/代码生成论文 | 新稿 |
30	| **Improve** | 改写已有章节，强化论证 | 修改稿 |
31	| **Audit** | 输出风险列表 + 缺失证据 + 修复建议 | 投前检查 |
32	
33	---
34	
35	## 2. Claim-Evidence 方法论
36	
37	**核心原则：论文是一个有中心贡献的故事，不是实验的堆砌。**
38	
39	### 2.1 一句话贡献
40	
41	在写任何内容之前，必须能用一句话说清贡献。如果不能，说明还没准备好写论文。
42	
43	### 2.2 Claim-Evidence Map
44	
45	为每个 contribution 建立 claim→evidence 映射表：
46	
47	| Contribution | Claim | Evidence | Table/Figure |
48	|-------------|-------|----------|-------------|
49	| C1: 方法 X 在 Y 上 SOTA | 比所有 baseline 高 ≥2% | 5-seed 平均，p<0.05 | Table 1 |
50	| C2: 消融证明 Z 是关键 | 去掉 Z 后掉 >5% | 消融实验 | Table 2 |
51	| C3: 计算效率 | 比 baseline 快 3× | FLOPs + wall-clock | Figure 3 |
52	
53	**没有直接证据支撑的 claim 必须删除或降级为 discussion。**
54	
55	### 2.3 章节起草顺序
56	
57	```
58	1. Introduction + Experiments（先确定 claim 和 evidence）
59	2. Method（评估问题稳定后再写细节）
60	3. Related Work
61	4. Abstract + Title（核心 claim 定型后最后写）
62	```
63	
64	### 2.4 Quality Gates
65	
66	投稿前必须通过：
67	- [ ] 前两段出现显式 novelty 声明
68	- [ ] 每个 contribution 有可验证的 evidence
69	- [ ] Abstract / Intro / Conclusion 的 claim 一致
70	- [ ] Limitations 真实且技术性诚实
71	- [ ] 审稿人两分钟内能识别 delta
72	
73	---
74	
75	## 3. 引用防幻觉（CRITICAL）
76	
77	**AI 生成引用有 ~40% 错误率。绝对禁止凭记忆写 BibTeX。**
78	
79	### 3.1 黄金法则
80	
81	```
82	IF 无法程序化获取引用:
83	    → 标记 [CITATION NEEDED] 或 \cite{PLACEHOLDER_verify}
84	    → 明确告知用户
85	    → 绝不编造看似合理的引用
86	```
87	
88	### 3.2 五步验证流程
89	
90	```
91	1. SEARCH  → Semantic Scholar API 搜索关键词
92	2. VERIFY  → 至少 2 个来源确认论文存在（S2 + arXiv/CrossRef）
93	3. RETRIEVE → 通过 DOI 获取 BibTeX（程序化，非记忆）
94	4. CHECK   → 确认你引用的 claim 确实出自该论文
95	5. ADD     → 加入 .bib 文件
96	```
97	
98	### 3.3 API 速查
99	
100	| API | 用途 | 限制 |
101	|-----|------|------|
102	| **Semantic Scholar** | ML/AI 论文搜索 | 1 RPS (免费) |
103	| **CrossRef** | DOI → BibTeX | 需 mailto 头 |
104	| **arXiv** | 预印本搜索 | 3s 间隔 |
105	
106	```python
107	# DOI → BibTeX（最可靠的方式）
108	import requests
109	def doi_to_bibtex(doi: str) -> str:
110	    r = requests.get(f"https://doi.org/{doi}",
111	                     headers={"Accept": "application/x-bibtex"})
112	    r.raise_for_status()
113	    return r.text
114	
115	# Semantic Scholar 搜索
116	from semanticscholar import SemanticScholar
117	sch = SemanticScholar()
118	results = sch.search_paper("attention mechanism transformers", limit=5)
119	for p in results:
120	    print(f"{p.title} | DOI: {p.externalIds.get('DOI', 'N/A')}")
121	```
122	
123	### 3.4 引用处理规则
124	
125	| 情况 | 处理 |
126	|------|------|
127	| 找到论文 + DOI + BibTeX | 直接用 |
128	| 找到论文，无 DOI | 用 arXiv BibTeX |
129	| 论文存在但无法获取 BibTeX | 标记 placeholder，告知用户 |
130	| 不确定论文是否存在 | 标记 `[CITATION NEEDED]`，告知用户 |
131	| "我觉得有篇关于 X 的论文" | **绝不引用** — 先搜索或标记 |
132

134	
135	## 4. 顶会模板速查
136	
137	### 4.1 ML/AI 会议
138	
139	| 会议 | 页数限制 | 主文件 | 样式文件 | 必须项 |
140	|------|---------|--------|---------|--------|
141	| **NeurIPS** | 9 页 | `main.tex` | `neurips.sty` | Paper Checklist |
142	| **ICML** | 8 页 (+1 camera-ready) | `example_paper.tex` | `icml2026.sty` | Broader Impact |
143	| **ICLR** | 9 页 (+1) | `iclr_conference.tex` | `iclr_conference.sty` | LLM 使用声明 |
144	| **ACL/EMNLP** | 8 页 (long) | `acl_latex.tex` | `acl.sty` | Limitations 必须 |
145	| **AAAI** | 7 页 (+1) | `aaai_template.tex` | `aaai.sty` | 严格样式 |
146	| **COLM** | 9 页 (+1) | `colm_conference.tex` | `colm_conference.sty` | 语言模型聚焦 |
147	
148	### 4.2 系统会议
149	
150	| 会议 | 页数限制 | 模板类型 | 关键要求 |
151	|------|---------|---------|---------|
152	| **OSDI** | 12 页 (+2) | USENIX `usenix.sty` | Research / Operational 双 track |
153	| **NSDI** | 12 页 | USENIX | Introduction 预筛 |
154	| **ASPLOS** | 12 页 | ACM `acmart.cls (sigplan)` | 前 2 页快速评审 |
155	| **SOSP** | 12 页 | ACM `acmart.cls (sigplan)` | 可选 Artifact Evaluation |
156	
157	### 4.3 模板使用原则
158	
159	```bash
160	# 1. 复制整个模板目录（不要只拷 .tex）
161	cp -r templates/neurips2025/ ~/papers/my-paper/
162	
163	# 2. 先编译未修改的模板，确认能跑通
164	latexmk -pdf main.tex
165	
166	# 3. 逐章替换内容，频繁编译
167	```
168	
169	**禁止事项：**
170	- 不要修改 `.sty` 文件
171	- 不要在会议模板间复制 `\usepackage` 行
172	- 不要只复制 `.tex` 而不带 `.sty/.bst`
173	
174	---
175	
176	## 5. 写作规范
177	
178	### 5.1 时间分配（Neel Nanda）
179	
180	花**大致相等**的时间在：
181	1. Abstract
182	2. Introduction
183	3. 图表
184	4. **其余所有内容加起来**
185	
186	审稿人读论文的顺序：标题 → 摘要 → 引言 → 图表 → 也许正文。
187	
188	### 5.2 Abstract 五句法（Sebastian Farquhar）
189	
190	```
191	1. 你做了什么（"We introduce/prove/demonstrate..."）
192	2. 为什么这很难且重要
193	3. 怎么做的（含专业关键词以便检索）
194	4. 有什么证据
195	5. 最惊人的数字/结果
196	```
197	
198	删除一切泛泛的开头：~~"Large language models have achieved remarkable success..."~~
199	
200	### 5.3 句子级清晰度（Gopen & Swan）
201	
202	| 原则 | 规则 |
203	|------|------|
204	| 主谓紧邻 | 不要在主语和动词之间插入长从句 |
205	| 重点在句尾 | 把关键数字/结论放在句末 |
206	| 旧信息在前 | 先放读者已知的内容，再引入新概念 |
207	| 动词表达动作 | "We analyzed" 而非 "We performed an analysis" |
208	
209	### 5.4 常见修改
210	
211	| 问题 | 修复 |
212	|------|------|
213	| Abstract 太泛 | 删第一句（如果它能放在任何 ML 论文前面） |
214	| Introduction 超 1.5 页 | 背景挪到 Related Work |
215	| 实验没有显式 claim | 每个实验前加 "This tests whether..." |
216	| 缺统计显著性 | 加 error bars + 运行次数 + 计算方法 |
217	
218	---
219	
220	## 6. 投稿工程链
221	
222	### 6.1 构建
223	
224	```bash
225	# 推荐：latexmk 一键构建
226	latexmk -pdf -interaction=nonstopmode -halt-on-error main.tex
227	
228	# 手动：需要多次编译
229	pdflatex main.tex && bibtex main && pdflatex main.tex && pdflatex main.tex
230	```
231	
232	### 6.2 Lint + 格式化
233	
234	```bash
235	# Lint: 检查常见 LaTeX 问题
236	chktex main.tex
237	
238	# 格式化: 减少 diff 噪声
239	latexindent -w main.tex
240	```
241	
242	### 6.3 图表格式
243	
244	| 类型 | 格式 | 说明 |
245	|------|------|------|
246	| 矢量图（plot、diagram） | PDF | matplotlib: `plt.savefig('fig.pdf')` |
247	| 照片/heatmap | PNG 600dpi 或 TIFF | 栅格图 |
248	| 表格 | `booktabs` 包 | `\toprule/\midrule/\bottomrule` |
249	
250	```latex
251	\usepackage{booktabs}
252	\begin{tabular}{lcc}
253	\toprule
254	Method & Accuracy $\uparrow$ & Latency $\downarrow$ \\
255	\midrule
256	Baseline & 85.2 & 45ms \\
257	\textbf{Ours} & \textbf{92.1} & 38ms \\
258	\bottomrule
259	\end{tabular}
260	```
261	
262	**图表规范：**
263	- 用色盲友好色板（Okabe-Ito 或 Paul Tol）
264	- 图内不放标题（用 caption）
265	- Caption 独立可理解（不依赖正文）
266	- 最佳值加粗，标注方向符号（↑ 越高越好，↓ 越低越好）
267	
268	### 6.4 投稿打包
269	
270	```bash
271	# 清理辅助文件
272	latexmk -c
273	
274	# 打包投稿
275	zip -r submission.zip main.tex *.sty *.bst *.bib figures/ --exclude='*.aux' --exclude='*.log'
276	```
277	
278	### 6.5 常见失败点
279	
280	| 问题 | 解决 |
281	|------|------|
282	| 图片格式混用导致编译失败 | 统一用 PDF |
283	| 字体未嵌入 | `pdffonts output.pdf` 检查 |
284	| Overfull box 过多 | 关键位置手动调行 |
285	| 引用缺字段 | 检查 DOI/年份/期刊名 |
286	| 超页数 | 用 `\vspace{-Xpt}` 微调，或精简内容 |
287	
288	---
289	
290	## 7. 会议转投
291	
292	当论文被拒或转投其他会议时：
293	
294	### 7.1 ML/AI 会议间转换
295	
296	| 转换 | 页数变化 | 关键调整 |
297	|------|---------|---------|
298	| NeurIPS → ICML | 9→8 | 删 1 页，加 Broader Impact |
299	| ICML → ICLR | 8→9 | 可扩展实验 + LLM 声明 |
300	| NeurIPS → ACL | 9→8 | 按 NLP 惯例重组 + Limitations |
301	| ICLR → AAAI | 9→7 | 大幅精简，严格样式 |
302	
303	### 7.2 转投原则
304	
305	1. **永远用目标模板重新开始** — 复制目标模板，迁移内容，不要合并 preamble
306	2. **只迁移正文内容** — 不复制 `\usepackage`、header/footer 命令
307	3. **根据审稿意见改进** — 但不要在新稿中提及旧投稿（双盲）
308	4. **检查目标会议特殊要求** — checklist、ethics statement、LLM 声明等
309	
310	---
311	
312	## 8. 投前清单
313	
314	### 通用清单
315	
316	- [ ] Abstract/Intro/Conclusion 的 claim 一致
317	- [ ] 每个 contribution 有对应 evidence
318	- [ ] 所有引用已程序化验证（无幻觉引用）
319	- [ ] Error bars + 运行次数 + 统计方法
320	- [ ] Limitations 章节存在且诚实
321	- [ ] 匿名化（双盲审稿）
322	- [ ] 符合页数限制
323	- [ ] 所有图表有自包含 caption
324	- [ ] `.bib` 中无缺失字段
325	- [ ] PDF 编译无 warning
326	
327	### 会议特定
328	
329	| 会议 | 额外要求 |
330	|------|---------|

Claim-Evidence 方法论\n\n**核心原则：论文是一个有中心贡献的故事，不是实验的堆砌。**\n\n### 2.1 一句话贡献\n\n在写任何内容之前，必须能用一句话说清贡献。如果不能，说明还没准备好写论文。\n\n### 2.2 Claim-Evidence Map\n\n为每个 contribution 建立 claim→evidence 映射表：\n\n| Contribution | Claim | Evidence | Table/Figure |\n|-------------|-------|----------|-------------|\n| C1: 方法 X 在 Y 上 SOTA | 比所有 baseline 高 ≥2% | 5-seed 平均，p<0.05 | Table 1 |\n| C2: 消融证明 Z 是关键 | 去掉 Z 后掉 >5% | 消融实验 | Table 2 |\n| C3: 计算效率 | 比 baseline 快 3× | FLOPs + wall-clock | Figure 3 |\n\n**没有直接证据支撑的 claim 必须删除或降级为 discussion。**\n\n### 2.3 章节起草顺序\n\n```\n1. Introduction + Experiments（先确定 claim 和 evidence）\n2. Method（评估问题稳定后再写细节）\n3. Related Work\n4. Abstract + Title（核心 claim 定型后最后写）\n```\n\n### 2.4 Quality Gates\n\n投稿前必须通过：\n- [ ] 前两段出现显式 novelty 声明\n- [ ] 每个 contribution 有可验证的 evidence\n- [ ] Abstract / Intro / Conclusion 的 claim 一致\n- [ ] Limitations 真实且技术性诚实\n- [ ] 审稿人两分钟内能识别 delta\n\n---\n\n## 3. 引用防幻觉（CRITICAL）\n\n**AI 生成引用有 ~40% 错误率。绝对禁止凭记忆写 BibTeX。**\n\n### 3.1 黄金法则\n\n```\nIF 无法程序化获取引用:\n    → 标记 [CITATION NEEDED] 或 \\cite{PLACEHOLDER_verify}\n    → 明确告知用户\n    → 绝不编造看似合理的引用\n```\n\n### 3.2 五步验证流程\n\n```\n1. SEARCH  → Semantic Scholar API 搜索关键词\n2. VERIFY  → 至少 2 个来源确认论文存在（S2 + arXiv/CrossRef）\n3. RETRIEVE → 通过 DOI 获取 BibTeX（程序化，非记忆）\n4. CHECK   → 确认你引用的 claim 确实出自该论文\n5. ADD     → 加入 .bib 文件\n```\n\n### 3.3 API 速查\n\n| API | 用途 | 限制 |\n|-----|------|------|\n| **Semantic Scholar** | ML/AI 论文搜索 | 1 RPS (免费) |\n| **CrossRef** | DOI → BibTeX | 需 mailto 头 |\n| **arXiv** | 预印本搜索 | 3s 间隔 |\n\n```python\n# DOI → BibTeX（最可靠的方式）\nimport requests\ndef doi_to_bibtex(doi: str) -> str:\n    r = requests.get(f\"https://doi.org/{doi}\",\n                     headers={\"Accept\": \"application/x-bibtex\"})\n    r.raise_for_status()\n    return r.text\n\n# Semantic Scholar 搜索\nfrom semanticscholar import SemanticScholar\nsch = SemanticScholar()\nresults = sch.search_paper(\"attention mechanism transformers\", limit=5)\nfor p in results:\n    print(f\"{p.title} | DOI: {p.externalIds.get('DOI', 'N/A')}\")\n```\n\n### 3.4 引用处理规则\n\n| 情况 | 处理 |\n|------|------|\n| 找到论文 + DOI + BibTeX | 直接用 |\n| 找到论文，无 DOI | 用 arXiv BibTeX |\n| 论文存在但无法获取 BibTeX | 标记 placeholder，告知用户 |\n| 不确定论文是否存在 | 标记 `[CITATION NEEDED]`，告知用户 |\n| \"我觉得有篇关于 X 的论文\" | **绝不引用** — 先搜索或标记 |\n\n---\n\n## 4. 顶会模板速查\n\n### 4.1 ML/AI 会议\n\n| 会议 | 页数限制 | 主文件 | 样式文件 | 必须项 |\n|------|---------|--------|---------|--------|\n| **NeurIPS** | 9 页 | `main.tex` | `neurips.sty` | Paper Checklist |\n| **ICML** | 8 页 (+1 camera-ready) | `example_paper.tex` | `icml2026.sty` | Broader Impact |\n| **ICLR** | 9 页 (+1) | `iclr_conference.tex` | `iclr_conference.sty` | LLM 使用声明 |\n| **ACL/EMNLP** | 8 页 (long) | `acl_latex.tex` | `acl.sty` | Limitations 必须 |\n| **AAAI** | 7 页 (+1) | `aaai_template.tex` | `aaai.sty` | 严格样式 |\n| **COLM** | 9 页 (+1) | `colm_conference.tex` | `colm_conference.sty` | 语言模型聚焦 |\n\n### 4.2 系统会议\n\n| 会议 | 页数限制 | 模板类型 | 关键要求 |\n|------|---------|---------|---------|\n| **OSDI** | 12 页 (+2) | USENIX `usenix.sty` | Research / Operational 双 track |\n| **NSDI** | 12 页 | USENIX | Introduction 预筛 |\n| **ASPLOS** | 12 页 | ACM `acmart.cls (sigplan)` | 前 2 页快速评审 |\n| **SOSP** | 12 页 | ACM `acmart.cls (sigplan)` | 可选 Artifact Evaluation |\n\n### 4.3 模板使用原则\n\n```bash\n# 1. 复制整个模板目录（不要只拷 .tex）\ncp -r templates/neurips2025/ ~/papers/my-paper/\n\n# 2. 先编译未修改的模板，确认能跑通\nlatexmk -pdf main.tex\n\n# 3. 逐章替换内容，频繁编译\n```\n\n**禁止事项：**\n- 不要修改 `.sty` 文件\n- 不要在会议模板间复制 `\\usepackage` 行\n- 不要只复制 `.tex` 而不带 `.sty/.bst`\n\n---\n\n## 5. 写作规范\n\n### 5.1 时间分配（Neel Nanda）\n\n花**大致相等**的时间在：\n1. Abstract\n2. Introduction\n3. 图表\n4. **其余所有内容加起来**\n\n审稿人读论文的顺序：标题 → 摘要 → 引言 → 图表 → 也许正文。\n\n### 5.2 Abstract 五句法（Sebastian Farquhar）\n\n```\n1. 你做了什么（\"We introduce/prove/demonstrate...\"）\n2. 为什么这很难且重要\n3. 怎么做的（含专业关键词以便检索）\n4. 有什么证据\n5. 最惊人的数字/结果\n```\n\n删除一切泛泛的开头：~~\"Large language models have achieved remarkable success...\"~~\n\n### 5.3 句子级清晰度（Gopen & Swan）\n\n| 原则 | 规则 |\n|------|------|\n| 主谓紧邻 | 不要在主语和动词之间插入长从句 |\n| 重点在句尾 | 把关键数字/结论放在句末 |\n| 旧信息在前 | 先放读者已知的内容，再引入新概念 |\n| 动词表达动作 | \"We analyzed\" 而非 \"We performed an analysis\" |\n\n### 5.4 常见修改\n\n| 问题 | 修复 |\n|------|------|\n| Abstract 太泛 | 删第一句（如果它能放在任何 ML 论文前面） |\n| Introduction 超 1.5 页 | 背景挪到 Related Work |\n| 实验没有显式 claim | 每个实验前加 \"This tests whether...\" |\n| 缺统计显著性 | 加 error bars + 运行次数 + 计算方法 |\n\n---\n\n## 6. 投稿工程链\n\n### 6.1 构建\n\n```bash\n# 推荐：latexmk 一键构建\nlatexmk -pdf -interaction=nonstopmode -halt-on-error main.tex\n\n# 手动：需要多次编译\npdflatex main.tex && bibtex main && pdflatex main.tex && pdflatex main.tex\n```\n\n### 6.2 Lint + 格式化\n\n```bash\n# Lint: 检查常见 LaTeX 问题\nchktex main.tex\n\n# 格式化: 减少 diff 噪声\nlatexindent -w main.tex\n```\n\n### 6.3 图表格式\n\n| 类型 | 格式 | 说明 |\n|------|------|------|\n| 矢量图（plot、diagram） | PDF | matplotlib: `plt.savefig('fig.pdf')` |\n| 照片/heatmap | PNG 600dpi 或 TIFF | 栅格图 |\n| 表格 | `booktabs` 包 | `\\toprule/\\midrule/\\bottomrule` |\n\n```latex\n\\usepackage{booktabs}\n\\begin{tabular}{lcc}\n\\toprule\nMethod & Accuracy $\\uparrow$ & Latency $\\downarrow$ \\\\\n\\midrule\nBaseline & 85.2 & 45ms \\\\\n\\textbf{Ours} & \\textbf{92.1} & 38ms \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n```\n\n**图表规范：**\n- 用色盲友好色板（Okabe-Ito 或 Paul Tol）\n- 图内不放标题（用 caption）\n- Caption 独立可理解（不依赖正文）\n- 最佳值加粗，标注方向符号（↑ 越高越好，↓ 越低越好）\n\n### 6.4 投稿打包\n\n```bash\n# 清理辅助文件\nlatexmk -c\n\n# 打包投稿\nzip -r submission.zip main.tex *.sty *.bst *.bib figures/ --exclude='*.aux' --exclude='*.log'\n```\n\n### 6.5 常见失败点\n\n| 问题 | 解决 |\n|------|------|\n| 图片格式混用导致编译失败 | 统一用 PDF |\n| 字体未嵌入 | `pdffonts output.pdf` 检查 |\n| Overfull box 过多 | 关键位置手动调行 |\n| 引用缺字段 | 检查 DOI/年份/期刊名 |\n| 超页数 | 用 `\\vspace{-Xpt}` 微调，或精简内容 |\n\n---\n\n## 7. 会议转投\n\n当论文被拒或转投其他会议时：\n\n### 7.1 ML/AI 会议间转换\n\n| 转换 | 页数变化 | 关键调整 |\n|------|---------|---------|\n| NeurIPS → ICML | 9→8 | 删 1 页，加 Broader Impact |\n| ICML → ICLR | 8→9 | 可扩展实验 + LLM 声明 |\n| NeurIPS → ACL | 9→8 | 按 NLP 惯例重组 + Limitations |\n| ICLR → AAAI | 9→7 | 大幅精简，严格样式 |\n\n### 7.2 转投原则\n\n1. **永远用目标模板重新开始** — 复制目标模板，迁移内容，不要合并 preamble\n2. **只迁移正文内容** — 不复制 `\\usepackage`、header/footer 命令\n3. **根据审稿意见改进** — 但不要在新稿中提及旧投稿（双盲）\n4. **检查目标会议特殊要求** — checklist、ethics statement、LLM 声明等\n\n---\n\n## 8. 投前清单\n\n### 通用清单\n\n- [ ] Abstract/Intro/Conclusion 的 claim 一致\n- [ ] 每个 contribution 有对应 evidence\n- [ ] 所有引用已程序化验证（无幻觉引用）\n- [ ] Error bars + 运行次数 + 统计方法\n- [ ] Limitations 章节存在且诚实\n- [ ] 匿名化（双盲审稿）\n- [ ] 符合页数限制\n- [ ] 所有图表有自包含 caption\n- [ ] `.bib` 中无缺失字段\n- [ ] PDF 编译无 warning\n\n### ```text
1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We evaluate STA-derived ISR across three axes: as a diagnostic for reward hacking (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic}), as a training reward exhibiting meta-level Goodhart's Law (\S\ref{sec:goodhart}), and as a cross-logic diagnostic protocol (\S\ref{sec:crosslogic}). We then synthesize the observed hacking strategies into a taxonomy (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy}).
5	
6	\subsection{Setup}
7	\label{sec:setup}
8	
9	\paragraph{Model and Training.}
10	We use Qwen3-4B as the base model, trained with GRPO~\cite{deepseekmath} using $K{=}8$ rollouts per prompt for 500 steps (default) or 1000 steps where noted. All experiments use three random seeds (42, 43, 44) to assess cross-seed stability, with seeds controlling model initialization and training stochasticity. Training data is a fixed 500-example subset of FOLIO~\cite{folio} (\texttt{train\_500\_seed42.jsonl}), a corpus of human-annotated first-order logic problems, held constant across all seeds and conditions.
11	
12	\paragraph{Solvers and Logic Types.}
13	For first-order logic (FOL), we use Prover9 as the automated theorem prover. For Prolog experiments, we use SWI-Prolog 8.4.2. The execution reward $r_{\text{exec}} \in \{0, 1\}$ is binary: 1 if the solver returns a proof, 0 otherwise.
14	
15	\paragraph{Evaluation.}
16	We evaluate on a held-out set of 100 FOLIO examples using greedy decoding. All ISR values reported in cross-run comparisons are computed by \texttt{eval\_sta.py}, which applies the full STA protocol uniformly. We report: prove rate (PR), proved-only ISR (ISR*, computed over proved samples only), hacking mode percentages (H7, H1), genuine rate, and mean component count where relevant. All tabulated ISR values are proved-only unless otherwise noted.
17	
18	\paragraph{Reward Formulations.}
19	We compare six conditions:
20	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
21	  \item \textbf{Exec-only}: $r = r_{\text{exec}}$ (baseline)
22	  \item \textbf{ISR $\beta$}: $r = r_{\text{exec}} \cdot (1 + \beta \cdot \text{ISR})$, with $\beta \in \{0.1, 0.3\}$
23	  \item \textbf{Random $\beta$}: $r = r_{\text{exec}} \cdot (1 + \beta \cdot u)$, $u \sim \mathcal{U}(0,1)$, matching ISR's range but providing no structural signal
24	  \item \textbf{SFT}: Standard supervised fine-tuning on gold formalizations (no RL)
25	\end{itemize}
26	
27	\subsection{ISR as Diagnostic}
28	\label{sec:diagnostic}
29	
30	\begin{figure}[t]
31	\centering
32	% Placeholder: dual-axis line plot
33	% X: training steps (100, 500, 1000). Left Y: PR (rising). Right Y: ISR (falling).
34	% Annotate component count trend and ISR=0 ratio at 1000 steps.
35	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/fig2_isr_collapse.pdf}
36	\caption{ISR collapse trajectory under exec-only GRPO training (seed 42). Prove rate rises from 0.32 to 0.96, while ISR falls from 0.642 to 0.003. By 1000 steps, 94.8\% of proved samples have ISR$=$0. Mean component count inflates from 6.58 (500 steps) to 18.55 (1000 steps), indicating contradiction explosion.}
37	\label{fig:isr-collapse}
38	\end{figure}
39	
40	We first establish that ISR detects structural degeneration invisible to prove rate. Figure~\ref{fig:isr-collapse} shows the ISR collapse trajectory under exec-only GRPO training for seed 42. At 100 training steps, the model produces structurally sound formalizations: PR$=$0.32, proved-only ISR$=$0.642. By 500 steps, PR climbs to 0.93, but ISR collapses to 0.132---87.1\% of proved samples embed the conclusion among the premises (H1 hacking), inflating prove rate without genuine reasoning. At 1000 steps, the collapse deepens: PR reaches 0.96 while ISR drops to 0.003, with 94.8\% of proved samples scoring ISR$=$0. The mean component count inflates from 6.58 to 18.55, reflecting a \emph{contradiction explosion} strategy where the model generates excessive premises that trivially entail any conclusion.
41	
42	\paragraph{Cross-Seed Instability.}
43	The three exec-only seeds diverge radically, underscoring that single-seed evaluation is unreliable for GRPO-trained formalizers. Seed 42 achieves high PR (0.93) via conclusion embedding. Seed 43 collapses to PR$=$0.14 with ISR$=$0 through repetition collapse (87\% of outputs hit the maximum token length). Seed 44 reaches PR$=$0.98 but through tautological collapse: the model generates two-component formalizations where the sole premise equals the conclusion (H7$=$100\%, mean components$=$2.04). Despite radically different PR values (0.14--0.98), all three seeds exhibit structural degeneration detectable by ISR. The cross-seed PR standard deviation of $\pm$0.47 is itself a finding: GRPO exec-only training is highly unstable, and aggregate metrics obscure qualitatively different failure modes.
44	
45	\paragraph{SFT Baseline.}
46	To confirm that hacking is RL-specific rather than a general fine-tuning artifact, we train a supervised baseline on gold formalizations. SFT achieves PR$=$0.14 with proved-only ISR$=$0.571, and 92.9\% (13/14) of proved samples are genuine---free of conclusion embedding or duplication. Only 1 of 14 proved samples exhibits H1 hacking (7.1\%), and no H7 instances occur. This confirms that the structural degeneration documented above is a consequence of RL reward optimization, not model capacity or data limitations.
47	
48	\subsection{Meta-Level Goodhart's Law}
49	\label{sec:goodhart}
50	
51	Having established ISR as a diagnostic, we test whether it can serve as a training signal. The answer is negative: ISR-as-reward exhibits \emph{meta-level} Goodhart's Law~\cite{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy}---the diagnostic metric itself becomes the target of gaming when optimized.
52	
53	\begin{table}[t]
54	\centering
55	\small
56	\begin{tabular}{llcccc}
57	\toprule
58	\textbf{Condition} & $\boldsymbol{\beta}$ & \textbf{PR}$\uparrow$ & \textbf{ISR}$\uparrow$ & \textbf{H7\%} & \textbf{Genuine\%} \\
59	\midrule
60	Exec-only & --- & 0.93 & 0.132 & 0 & 12.9 \\
61	SFT & --- & 0.14 & 0.571$^*$ & 0 & \textbf{92.9} \\
62	\midrule
63	ISR & 0.1 & 0.77{\scriptsize$\pm$.14} & 0.716{\scriptsize$\pm$.26}$^*$ & \textbf{64.5} & $\sim$16 \\
64	Random & 0.1 & 0.85{\scriptsize$\pm$.06} & 0.086{\scriptsize$\pm$.04}$^*$ & 0 & 18.7 \\
65	\midrule
66	ISR & 0.3 & 0.18 & 0.143$^*$ & --- & --- \\
67	Random & 0.3 & 0.48 & 0.094$^*$ & --- & --- \\
68	\bottomrule
69	\end{tabular}
70	\caption{Goodhart landscape across reward conditions. ISR and Random rows at $\beta{=}0.1$ report 3-seed mean$\pm$std; others are seed 42. ISR$^*$ denotes proved-only ISR. Exec-only and SFT are single-seed (s42). H7: single premise$=$conclusion (ISR-specific gaming). At $\beta{=}0.1$, ISR reward induces ISR-specific H7 gaming (64.5\% vs 0\%); at $\beta{=}0.3$, PR collapses (0.18 vs 0.48 random).}
71	\label{tab:goodhart-landscape}
72	\end{table}
73	
74	\paragraph{Low-Dose ISR ($\beta{=}0.1$): ISR-Specific Gaming.}
75	Table~\ref{tab:goodhart-landscape} summarizes results across conditions. At $\beta{=}0.1$, two of three ISR seeds (s43, s44) are dominated by H7 hacking---the model generates a single premise identical to the conclusion, trivially achieving both high prove rate and high ISR. Across the three ISR seeds, H7 accounts for 64.5\% of proved samples (149/231). In contrast, all three random control seeds show \emph{zero} H7 instances (0/256). H7 is thus exclusively induced by the ISR reward signal.
76	
77	\begin{table}[t]
78	\centering
79	\small
80	\setlength{\tabcolsep}{3.5pt}
81	\begin{tabular}{llccccc}
82	\toprule
83	\textbf{Seed} & \textbf{Cond.} & \textbf{PR} & \textbf{ISR}$^*$ & \textbf{H7\%} & \textbf{H1\%} & \textbf{Gen.\%} \\
84	\midrule
85	s42 & ISR & 0.81 & 0.415 & 16.0 & 51.9 & 30.9 \\
86	s43 & ISR & 0.61 & 0.830 & \textbf{96.7} & 0 & 3.3 \\
87	s44 & ISR & 0.89 & 0.902 & \textbf{86.5} & 0 & 13.5$^\dagger$ \\
88	\midrule
89	s42 & Rand & 0.79 & 0.131 & 0 & 70.9 & 29.1 \\
90	s43 & Rand & 0.88 & 0.058 & 0 & \textbf{93.2} & 6.8 \\
91	s44 & Rand & 0.89 & 0.070 & 0 & 79.8 & 20.2 \\
92	\bottomrule
93	\end{tabular}
94	\caption{ISR vs Random at $\beta{=}0.1$: 3-seed comparison. ISR$^*$: proved-only. H7 is ISR-specific (64.5\% mean vs 0\%). H1 (conclusion embedding) is GRPO-universal, higher under random (81.3\%) than ISR (17.3\%). $^\dagger$s44's 12 ``genuine'' samples are all single-premise (n\_prem$=$1), representing H7 classifier false negatives.}
95	\label{tab:beta01-3seed}
96	\end{table}
97	
98	Table~\ref{tab:beta01-3seed} provides the full 3-seed breakdown. Conclusion embedding (H1) is a GRPO-universal strategy: it appears at 70.9--93.2\% under random reward and 0--51.9\% under ISR. The ISR reward does not reduce total hacking---it \emph{converts} H1 hacking into the more extreme H7 form. Overall conclusion-in-premise rates are comparable (ISR s42: 67.9\% vs Random s42: 70.9\%).
99	
100	\paragraph{Premise-Count Confound.}
101	Seed 42 under ISR---the only seed not dominated by H7---shows higher raw proved-only ISR (0.415 vs 0.131 for random). However, this apparent advantage is entirely a premise minimization artifact. ISR-trained models produce shorter formalizations (mean 5.58 components vs 8.46 for random), and LOO-based metrics are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises mean each individual premise is more likely to be necessary, inflating ISR independent of reasoning quality.
102	
103	To control for this, we compare ISR values only among genuine (non-hacking) samples with $\geq$3 premises: ISR genuine achieves 0.258 ($n{=}11$) versus random genuine at 0.243 ($n{=}21$). The ratio is 1.06$\times$---no meaningful structural advantage. The previously reported 2.4$\times$ raw ISR difference reduces to noise once premise count is controlled.
104	
105	\paragraph{High-Dose ISR ($\beta{=}0.3$): Classic Goodhart.}
106	At $\beta{=}0.3$, ISR reward causes PR to collapse to 0.18---a 5.2$\times$ degradation from exec-only. The matched random control achieves PR$=$0.48, demonstrating that ISR at high dose is not merely unhelpful but \emph{actively harmful} relative to a structurally uninformative signal.
107	
108	\paragraph{Dose-Response Pattern.}
109	Together, the two dose levels reveal a complete Goodhart trajectory~\cite{karwowski-goodhart-rl}: at low dose ($\beta{=}0.1$), the model games the proxy metric itself (ISR inflation via H7), producing high ISR scores that mask structural degeneration; at high dose ($\beta{=}0.3$), the ISR signal overwhelms the execution reward, causing format collapse and PR degradation. No dose yields genuine structural improvement.
110	
111	\subsection{Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer}
112	\label{sec:crosslogic}
113	
114	To test whether ISR generalizes beyond a single formal language, we replicate the diagnostic and reward experiments using Prolog with SWI-Prolog 8.4.2 as the solver. Figure~\ref{fig:prolog-comparison} provides a direct visual comparison of FOL and Prolog exec-only ISR across all three seeds.
115	
116	\begin{figure}[t]
117	\centering
118	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/fig4_prolog_comparison.pdf}
119	\caption{FOL vs Prolog exec-only ISR comparison (3 seeds). Both logic types yield near-zero ISR under exec-only training, confirming cross-logic diagnostic transfer. Seed 44 FOL (ISR$=$0.58) is a tautological artifact ($\dagger$). PR values annotated above each bar.}
120	\label{fig:prolog-comparison}
121	\end{figure}
122	
123	\begin{table}[t]
124	\centering
125	\small
126	\setlength{\tabcolsep}{3pt}
127	\begin{tabular}{llcccc}
128	\toprule
129	\textbf{Logic} & \textbf{Condition} & \textbf{Seed} & \textbf{PR} & \textbf{ISR*} & \textbf{Mode} \\
130	\midrule
131	\multicolumn{6}{l}{\textit{Exec-only (diagnostic transfer)}} \\
132	FOL & Exec & s42 & 0.93 & 0.132 & H1 embedding \\
133	FOL & Exec & s43 & 0.14 & 0 & Repetition \\
134	FOL & Exec & s44 & 0.98 & 0.582$^\ddagger$ & Tautological \\
135	\cmidrule{1-6}
136	Prolog & Exec & s42 & 0.83 & 0.037 & Ground facts \\
137	Prolog & Exec & s43 & 0.56 & 0.015 & Degraded \\
138	Prolog & Exec & s44 & 0.21 & 0.387 & Degraded \\
139	\midrule
140	\multicolumn{6}{l}{\textit{ISR-as-reward (Goodhart transfer)}} \\
141	Prolog & STA & s42 & 0.50 & 1.000 & H7 100\% \\
142	Prolog & STA & s43 & 0.38 & 0.185 & Degen.\ syntax$^\S$ \\
143	Prolog & STA & s44 & 0.97 & 0.912 & Query-as-fact$^\P$ \\
144	\bottomrule
145	\end{tabular}
146	\caption{Cross-logic transfer (proved-only ISR). \textit{Top}: Exec-only results for FOL and Prolog (3 seeds each). Prolog exec-only ISR* near-zero for 2/3 seeds (s42$=$0.037, s43$=$0.015); s44 ISR*$=$0.387 reflects small-sample artifact ($n{=}21$ proved out of 100, high variance). \textit{Bottom}: Prolog STA (ISR-as-reward) produces universal Goodhart---3/3 seeds gaming, 0\% genuine. $^\ddagger$Tautological: premise$=$conclusion, ISR inflated by design. $^\S$Functional H7: uppercase pseudo-Prolog, $n_{\text{necessary}}{=}1$ for 38/38 proved. $^\P$Query-as-fact: NL$\to$snake\_case atom set as both fact and query; $n_{\text{necessary}}{=}1$ for 95/97.}
147	\label{tab:prolog-crosslogic}
148	\end{table}
149	
150	\paragraph{Diagnostic Transfer.}
151	Table~\ref{tab:prolog-crosslogic} (top) shows that ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog just as it does in FOL. Two of three Prolog exec-only seeds yield proved-only ISR* near zero (s42$=$0.037, s43$=$0.015), confirming structurally degenerate outputs. The third seed (s44, ISR*$=$0.387) is a small-sample artifact: only 21 of 100 samples prove, and the higher ISR* reflects variance from this sparse denominator rather than genuine structural quality. The cross-seed PR instability observed in FOL ($\sigma{=}$0.47) replicates in Prolog ($\sigma{=}$0.31), and both logic types show qualitatively different collapse modes across seeds.
152	We note that low ISR in Prolog exec-only reflects redundant coding---repeated ground facts where removing any single copy does not affect the proof---rather than absence of reasoning per se. This contrasts with the high ISR under Prolog STA, which reflects metric gaming. ISR values must be interpreted in context of the training objective.
153	
154	\paragraph{Universal Goodhart in Prolog.}
155	Table~\ref{tab:prolog-crosslogic} (bottom) reveals the strongest Goodhart result: all three Prolog STA seeds converge to gaming, with 0\% genuine proofs across all 185 proved samples. Each seed develops a distinct gaming pathway:
156	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
157	  \item \textbf{s42}: Clean H7---the model generates a single Prolog clause identical to the query (50/50 proved, ISR*$=$1.000---every component is trivially necessary when there is only one).
158	  \item \textbf{s43}: Degenerate syntax---the model produces uppercase pseudo-Prolog (e.g., \texttt{MARY\_IS\_AN\_AVADEOTE.}). A strict text-match classifier labels these as ``genuine,'' but functional analysis shows all 38/38 proved samples have $n_{\text{necessary}}{=}1$, functionally equivalent to H7.
159	  \item \textbf{s44}: Query-as-fact gaming---the model converts NL problems to snake\_case atoms and sets them as both fact and query, achieving PR$=$0.97 with $n_{\text{necessary}}{=}1$ for 95/97 proved samples. Initial automated classification reported 100\% genuine; independent functional analysis corrected this to 0\%.
160	\end{itemize}
161	
162	This result is stronger than the FOL finding: FOL ISR $\beta{=}0.1$ retains $\sim$16\% genuine residual across seeds, while Prolog STA produces zero. Prolog's simpler semantics appear to enable more complete exploitation, eliminating the genuine residual observed in FOL.
163	
164	\paragraph{Classifier Limitations.}
165	The Prolog results expose a blind spot of text-match hacking classifiers. Seeds s43 and s44 bypass the strict H7 classifier through surface-level novelty (non-standard casing, atom-level encoding) while remaining functionally tautological ($n_{\text{necessary}}{=}1$). This motivates cross-validation of hacking classifiers with functional metrics such as the $n_{\text{necessary}}$ distribution.
166	
167	\subsection{Hacking Taxonomy}
168	\label{sec:taxonomy}
169	
170	\begin{figure}[t]
171	\centering
172	% Placeholder: stacked bar chart
173	% X-axis: Exec-only s42, ISR β=0.1 s42/s43/s44, Random β=0.1 (3-seed mean), SFT
174	% Stacked segments: H7 (red), H1 (orange), Other hacking (yellow), Genuine (green)
175	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/fig3_hacking_composition.pdf}
176	\caption{Hacking mode composition across reward conditions (proved samples). ISR reward converts H1 (conclusion embedding) into the more extreme H7 (conclusion duplication) without reducing total hacking. SFT exhibits near-zero hacking. ``Other'' includes complexity-without-utility and format artifacts.}
177	\label{fig:hacking-composition}
178	\end{figure}
179	
180	Across all conditions, seeds, and logic types, we identify seven distinct hacking modes (Figure~\ref{fig:hacking-composition}):
181	
182	\begin{enumerate}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
183	  \item \textbf{H1: Conclusion embedding.} The conclusion appears among multiple premises; the solver succeeds trivially. GRPO-universal: 52--93\% of proved samples across reward types.
184	  \item \textbf{H7: Conclusion duplication.} A single premise identical to the conclusion. ISR-specific: 64.5\% under ISR $\beta{=}0.1$ vs 0\% under random.
185	  \item \textbf{Tautological collapse.} Two-component formalizations where premise$=$conclusion (exec-only s44, 94\% of outputs).
186	  \item \textbf{Repetition collapse.} Identical premises repeated until the token limit (exec-only s43, 87\% hit max\_new\_tokens).
187	  \item \textbf{Complexity-without-utility.} Syntactically complex but semantically vacuous premises (random $\beta{=}0.3$).
188	  \item \textbf{Contradiction explosion.} Excessive premises (mean 18.55 components at 1000 steps) that trivially entail any conclusion.
189	  \item \textbf{Query-as-fact gaming.} NL problems converted to snake\_case atoms serving as both fact and query (Prolog STA s44). Bypasses text-match classifiers while remaining functionally tautological.
190	\end{enumerate}
191	
192	The reward signal determines hacking \emph{composition} but not \emph{prevalence}: total conclusion-in-premise rates are comparable across ISR and random conditions ($\sim$68--81\%), but ISR converts the GRPO-universal H1 mode into the ISR-specific H7 mode. SFT remains the only condition with near-zero hacking (92.9\% genuine), confirming that hacking is RL-reward-specific.
193

PR values annotated above each bar.}\n\\label{fig:prolog-comparison}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\setlength{\\tabcolsep}{3pt}\n\\begin{tabular}{llcccc}\n\\toprule\n\\textbf{Logic} & \\textbf{Condition} & \\textbf{Seed} & \\textbf{PR} & \\textbf{ISR*} & \\textbf{Mode} \\\\\n\\midrule\n\\multicolumn{6}{l}{\\textit{Exec-only (diagnostic transfer)}} \\\\\nFOL & Exec & s42 & 0.93 & 0.132 & H1 embedding \\\\\nFOL & Exec & s43 & 0.14 & 0 & Repetition \\\\\nFOL & Exec & s44 & 0.98 & 0.582$^\\ddagger$ & Tautological \\\\\n\\cmidrule{1-6}\nProlog & Exec & s42 & 0.83 & 0.037 & Ground facts \\\\\nProlog & Exec & s43 & 0.56 & 0.015 & Degraded \\\\\nProlog & Exec & s44 & 0.21 & 0.387 & Degraded \\\\\n\\midrule\n\\multicolumn{6}{l}{\\textit{ISR-as-reward (Goodhart transfer)}} \\\\\nProlog & STA & s42 & 0.50 & 1.000 & H7 100\\% \\\\\nProlog & STA & s43 & 0.38 & 0.185 & Degen.\\ syntax$^\\S$ \\\\\nProlog & STA & s44 & 0.97 & 0.912 & Query-as-fact$^\\P$ \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\caption{Cross-logic transfer (proved-only ISR). \\textit{Top}: Exec-only results for FOL and Prolog (3 seeds each). Prolog exec-only ISR* near-zero for 2/3 seeds (s42$=$0.037, s43$=$0.015); s44 ISR*$=$0.387 reflects small-sample artifact ($n{=}21$ proved out of 100, high variance). \\textit{Bottom}: Prolog STA (ISR-as-reward) produces universal Goodhart---3/3 seeds gaming, 0\\% genuine. $^\\ddagger$Tautological: premise$=$conclusion, ISR inflated by design. $^\\S$Functional H7: uppercase pseudo-Prolog, $n_{\\text{necessary}}{=}1$ for 38/38 proved. $^\\P$Query-as-fact: NL$\\to$snake\\_case atom set as both fact and query; $n_{\\text{necessary}}{=}1$ for 95/97.}\n\\label{tab:prolog-crosslogic}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\paragraph{Diagnostic Transfer.}\nTable~\\ref{tab:prolog-crosslogic} (top) shows that ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog just as it does in FOL. Two of three Prolog exec-only seeds yield proved-only ISR* near zero (s42$=$0.037, s43$=$0.015), confirming structurally degenerate outputs. The third seed (s44, ISR*$=$0.387) is a small-sample artifact: only 21 of 100 samples prove, and the higher ISR* reflects variance from this sparse denominator rather than genuine structural quality. The cross-seed PR instability observed in FOL ($\\sigma{=}$0.47) replicates in Prolog ($\\sigma{=}$0.31), and both logic types show qualitatively different collapse modes across seeds.\nWe note that low ISR in Prolog exec-only reflects redundant coding---repeated ground facts where removing any single copy does not affect the proof---rather than absence of reasoning per se. This contrasts with the high ISR under Prolog STA, which reflects metric gaming. ISR values must be interpreted in context of the training objective.\n\n\\paragraph{Universal Goodhart in Prolog.}\nTable~\\ref{tab:prolog-crosslogic} (bottom) reveals the strongest Goodhart result: all three Prolog STA seeds converge to gaming, with 0\\% genuine proofs across all 185 proved samples. Each seed develops a distinct gaming pathway:\n\\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]\n  \\item \\textbf{s42}: Clean H7---the model generates a single Prolog clause identical to the query (50/50 proved, ISR*$=$1.000---every component is trivially necessary when there is only one).\n  \\item \\textbf{s43}: Degenerate syntax---the model produces uppercase pseudo-Prolog (e.g., \\texttt{MARY\\_IS\\_AN\\_AVADEOTE.}). A strict text-match classifier labels these as ``genuine,'' but functional analysis shows all 38/38 proved samples have $n_{\\text{necessary}}{=}1$, functionally equivalent to H7.\n  \\item \\textbf{s44}: Query-as-fact gaming---the model converts NL problems to snake\\_case atoms and sets them as both fact and query, achieving PR$=$0.97 with $n_{\\text{necessary}}{=}1$ for 95/97 proved samples. Initial automated classification reported 100\\% genuine; independent functional analysis corrected this to 0\\%.\n\\end{itemize}\n\nThis result is stronger than the FOL finding: FOL ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$ retains $\\sim$16\\% genuine residual across seeds, while Prolog STA produces zero. Prolog's simpler semantics appear to enable more complete exploitation, eliminating the genuine residual observed in FOL.\n\n\\paragraph{Classifier Limitations.}\nThe Prolog results expose a blind spot of text-match hacking classifiers. Seeds s43 and s44 bypass the strict H7 classifier through surface-level novelty (non-standard casing, atom-level encoding) while remaining functionally tautological ($n_{\\text{necessary}}{=}1$). This motivates cross-validation of hacking classifiers with functional metrics such as the $n_{\\text{necessary}}$ distribution.\n\n\\subsection{Hacking Taxonomy}\n\\label{sec:taxonomy}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n% Placeholder: stacked bar chart\n% X-axis: Exec-only s42, ISR β=0.1 s42/s43/s44, Random β=0.1 (3-seed mean), SFT\n% Stacked segments: H7 (red), H1 (orange), Other hacking (yellow), Genuine (green)\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/fig3_hacking_composition.pdf}\n\\caption{Hacking mode composition across reward conditions (proved samples). ISR reward converts H1 (conclusion embedding) into the more extreme H7 (conclusion duplication) without reducing total hacking. SFT exhibits near-zero hacking. ``Other'' includes complexity-without-utility and format artifacts.}\n\\label{fig:hacking-composition}\n\\end{figure}\n\nAcross all conditions, seeds, and logic types, we identify seven distinct hacking modes (Figure~\\ref{fig:hacking-composition}):\n\n\\begin{enumerate}[nosep,leftmargin=*]\n  \\item \\textbf{H1: Conclusion embedding.} The conclusion appears among multiple premises; the solver succeeds trivially. GRPO-universal: 52--93\\% of proved samples across reward types.\n  \\item \\textbf{H7: Conclusion duplication.} A single premise identical to the conclusion. ISR-specific: 64.5\\% under ISR $\\beta{=}0.1$ vs 0\\% under random.\n  \\item \\textbf{Tautological collapse.} Two-component formalizations where premise$=$conclusion (exec-only s44, 94\\% of outputs).\n  \\item \\textbf{Repetition collapse.} Identical premises repeated until the token limit (exec-only s43, 87\\% hit max\\_new\\_tokens).\n  \\item \\textbf{Complexity-without-utility.} Syntactically complex but semantically vacuous premises (random $\\beta{=}0.3$).\n  \\item \\textbf{Contradiction explosion.} Excessive premises (mean 18.55 components at 1000 steps) that trivially entail any conclusion.\n  \\item \\textbf{Query-as-fact gaming.} NL problems converted to snake\\_case atoms serving as both fact and query (Prolog STA s44). Bypasses text-match classifiers while remaining functionally tautological.\n\\end{enumerate}\n\nThe reward signal determines hacking \\emph{composition} but not \\emph{prevalence}: total conclusion-in-premise rates are comparable across ISR and random conditions ($\\sim$68--81\\%), but ISR converts the GRPO-universal H1 mode into the ISR-specific H7 mode. SFT remains the only condition with near-zero hacking (92.9\\% genuine), confirming that hacking is RL-reward-specific.\n",

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/writing_plan.yaml\", \"content\": \"meta:\\n  venue: EMNLP 2026\\n  track: main\\n  template: acl2023/\\n  template_note: \\\"ARR May 25 cycle. ACL Rolling Review format (acl2023.sty).\\\"\\n  page_limit: 8\\n  deadline: 2026-05-25\\n  core_claim: >\\n    ISR (Intensional Structural Reward), derived via Solver Trace Ablation,\\n    serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers\\n    but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law — the diagnostic\\n    metric becomes gamed when optimized, with apparent improvements explained by\\n    premise minimization artifacts.\\n  created_at: 2026-05-14T18:00:00Z\\n  updated_at: 2026-05-14T19:05:00Z\\n  update_note: \\\"Director approved with 5 corrections applied: taxonomy 6→7, Claim 3 Prolog STA 3/3 gaming complete, conclusion limitations updated, tab5/fig4 expanded, registry_refs added.\\\"\\n\\npaper_outline:\\n  - section: abstract\\n    word_budget: 250\\n    core_bullets:\\n      - \\\"Problem: GRPO formalizers achieve high prove rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs; no diagnostic exists\\\"\\n      - \\\"Method: STA derives ISR via leave-one-out component necessity testing on solver inputs\\\"\\n      - \\\"Claim 1: ISR detects hacking invisible to prove_rate (0.64→0.13→0.003 collapse over 100→500→1000 steps)\\\"\\n      - \\\"Claim 2: ISR-as-reward exhibits meta-level Goodhart — β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming (H7=64.5% vs 0% random), premise-matched 1.06x; β=0.3 PR collapses (18% vs 48% random)\\\"\\n      - \\\"Claim 3: ISR diagnostic transfers to Prolog (cross-logic generality)\\\"\\n      - \\\"Taxonomy: 7 distinct hacking modes identified across conditions (including query-as-fact gaming discovered in Prolog STA)\\\"\\n    dependencies: [experiments, method, discussion]\\n    tables: []\\n    figures: []\\n    registry_refs: []\\n\\n  - section: introduction\\n    word_budget: 1200\\n    core_bullets:\\n      - \\\"Hook: A GRPO formalizer achieves 93% prove rate — but 87% of its proved outputs embed the conclusion in the premises. Structural shortcuts invisible to execution reward.\\\"\\n      - \\\"Gap: Execution-only evaluation (prove_rate) conflates genuine reasoning with degenerate shortcuts. No principled diagnostic exists for structural integrity in neurosymbolic GRPO.\\\"\\n      - \\\"Insight: Component necessity testing (LOO ablation on solver inputs) yields ISR — AND when this diagnostic becomes reward, it exhibits meta-level Goodhart: the model games the metric itself.\\\"\\n      - \\\"Mechanism: STA protocol + systematic study across 6 reward conditions × 3 seeds × up to 1000 steps\\\"\\n      - \\\"Contributions: (C1) ISR as diagnostic with complete collapse trajectory; (C2) Meta-level Goodhart empirical characterization with premise-count confound analysis; (C3) Cross-logic diagnostic transfer; (C4) 7-mode hacking taxonomy\\\"\\n    dependencies: [experiments]\\n    citation_hints:\\n      - \\\"GRPO: deepseekmath\\\"\\n      - \\\"Neurosymbolic: satlm, logic-lm, linc\\\"\\n      - \\\"Reward hacking: gao-reward-overoptimization, karwowski-goodhart-rl\\\"\\n      - \\\"Goodhart taxonomy: manheim-goodhart-taxonomy\\\"\\n    figures: [fig1_sta_overview]\\n    tables: []\\n    registry_refs: []\\n\\n  - section: related_work\\n    word_budget: 900\\n    core_bullets:\\n      - \\\"Cluster 1 — Neurosymbolic Reasoning with LLMs: Logic-LM, LINC, SatLM, LoRP, FoVer pipeline approach (prompting); Mellgren Prolog GRPO (RL). STA differs: structural reward + diagnostic focus\\\"\\n      - \\\"Cluster 2 — Reward Shaping & Process Supervision: Lightman verify-step, Math-Shepherd, RLSF symbolic feedback, Rubric-GRPO. STA's ISR is solver-grounded process signal; our finding: process reward can itself Goodhart\\\"\\n      - \\\"Cluster 3 — Reward Hacking & Goodhart's Law: Gao scaling laws, Karwowski geometric, Manheim taxonomy, Skalse characterization, Pan misspecification. We contribute empirical Goodhart in formal reasoning + meta-level (proxy games itself)\\\"\\n      - \\\"Cluster 4 — Mutation Testing & Component Analysis: DeMillo 1978, Jia-Harman survey, Just mutants. STA adapts LOO mutation from SE to formal logic\\\"\\n    dependencies: [introduction]\\n    citation_hints:\\n      - \\\"Must cite: deepseekmath, logic-lm, linc, satlm, mellgren-prolog-grpo\\\"\\n      - \\\"Must cite: lightman-verify-step, gao-reward-overoptimization, karwowski-goodhart-rl, manheim-goodhart-taxonomy\\\"\\n      - \\\"Must cite: demillo1978, folio, proofwriter\\\"\\n    figures: []\\n    tables: []\\n    registry_refs: []\\n\\n  - section: method\\n    word_budget: 1200\\n    core_bullets:\\n      - \\\"3.1 Task: NL → Formal Logic formalization, verified by external solver (Prover9 for FOL, SWI-Prolog for Prolog)\\\"\\n      - \\\"3.2 GRPO Training: execution-only reward r_exec ∈ {0,1}, group relative policy optimization\\\"\\n      - \\\"3.3 Solver Trace Ablation (STA): LOO ablation protocol — for proved sample with n components, remove each component, re-run solver, ISR = fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure\\\"\\n      - \\\"3.4 ISR Definition: ISR(x) = (1/n) Σ_i 1[solver fails on x\\\\\\\\{c_i}], bounded [0,1]. ISR=1 → all necessary; ISR=0 → none necessary\\\"\\n      - \\\"3.5 Reward Formulations: exec-only, additive ISR (r_exec·(1+β·ISR)), additive random control, convex combination, SFT baseline\\\"\\n      - \\\"3.6 Hacking Taxonomy: 7 modes — H1 (multi-premise conclusion embedding), H7 (single premise = conclusion), tautological collapse, repetition collapse, complexity-without-utility, contradiction explosion, query-as-fact gaming (Prolog STA s44, bypasses text-match classifier)\\\"\\n    dependencies: []\\n    notation_table:\\n      r_exec: \\\"binary execution reward (solver success)\\\"\\n      ISR: \\\"Intensional Structural Reward\\\"\\n      beta: \\\"ISR reward weight\\\"\\n      n: \\\"number of components in formalization\\\"\\n      c_i: \\\"i-th component (premise/rule)\\\"\\n      H1: \\\"multi-premise conclusion embedding\\\"\\n      H7: \\\"single premise = conclusion\\\"\\n    figures: [fig1_sta_overview]\\n    tables: []\\n    registry_refs: [sta-phase0-baseline, plan003-eval-exec-only-r5]\\n\\n  - section: experiments\\n    word_budget: 2800\\n    core_bullets:\\n      - \\\"4.1 Setup: Qwen3-4B, GRPO (K=8, 500 steps default), ProofWriter-FOL-100 eval, Prover9/SWI-Prolog solvers, 3 seeds (42/43/44)\\\"\\n      - \\\"4.2 ISR as Diagnostic (Claim 1): ISR collapse trajectory 0.6423→0.1324→0.0027 over 100→500→1000 steps (Fig2 with embedded data); s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\\n- **不是 \\\"seed-mode separation\\\"（旧版误报）——是 \\\"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\\\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \\\"hacking 不是必然\\\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\\n\\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=64.5% (3-seed mean), H1=17.3%, Genuine=~16%（模式混合，有 residual genuine）\\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~16% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\\n\\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \\\"genuine improvement\\\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse (PR 18% vs random 48%)\\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (7 modes identified, including ISR-specific \\\"conclusion duplication with ISR inflation\\\" and Prolog-specific \\\"query-as-fact gaming\\\")\\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\\n  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual\\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \\\"genuine improvement\\\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\\n- **Headline result**: \\\"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds via conclusion duplication that inflates ISR itself; the remaining seed's apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse occurs (PR 18% vs random 48%). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~16% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 7 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\\\"\\n\\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\\n\\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\\n\\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\\n\\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\\n\\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\\\"必要\\\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\\n\\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051→D057 闭环)\\n\\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 |\\n|------|------|---------|----------|----------|---------------|\\n| exec-only (baseline) | r = r_exec | 0.93 (s42) | 0.123 | H1=87.1%, conclusion embedding (diluted) | N/A |\\n| SFT baseline | supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |\\n| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |\\n| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\\n| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | **0.902** | **H7 87%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\\n| random β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.79 | 0.131 | H1 71%, redundant premises | control |\\n| random β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.88 | 0.058 | H1 93%, redundant premises | control |\\n| random β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.89 | 0.070 | H1 80%, redundant premises | control |\\n| ISR β=0.3 | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·ISR | 0.77 (train) | 0.230 (train) | ISR-PR separation | penalty effect |\\n| convex random | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·random | 1.00 | 0.535 (fake) | tautological | hacking |\\n\\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 是 classic Goodhart。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\\n\\n## Venue Fit Risks\\n\\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.\\n- **\\\"Just negative results\\\" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.\\n- **\\\"Old wine\\\" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.\\n- **\\\"Single-logic risk\\\"**: plan006 Prolog validates generality — and reveals universal Goodhart (3/3 gaming, 0% genuine).\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n1. **\\\"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel\\\"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.\\n2. **\\\"β=0.1 success is just one seed\\\"** — D051 shows even that one seed's \\\"success\\\" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.\\n3. **\\\"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results\\\"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-93%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-93% vs 17-52%).\\n4. **\\\"Goodhart's Law is obvious\\\"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.\\n5. **\\\"No positive result\\\"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).\\n6. **\\\"ISR is circular\\\"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\\n7. **\\\"Only one logic type\\\"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality with even stronger results (3/3 Goodhart, 0% genuine vs FOL ~16%).\\n8. **\\\"500 steps too short\\\"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).\\n9. **\\\"3 seeds insufficient\\\"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.\\n10. **\\\"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern\\\"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.\\n11. **\\\"Premise count confound is obvious\\\"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.\\n12. **\\\"Practitioners can't act on this\\\"** — 7 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable. Classifier cross-validation with n_necessary is a concrete tool.\\n13. **\\\"Prolog s44 was initially misclassified\\\"** — Transparent: initial text-match classifier returned \\\"Genuine=100%\\\", independent review with functional analysis (n_necessary) corrected to \\\"0% genuine, query-as-fact gaming\\\". Critically, Prolog STA reveals **universal Goodhart across all seeds**: identical reward signal + identical model → each seed converges to a distinct **gaming** mode (H7 conclusion duplication / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact gaming), with **3/3 seeds exhibiting Goodhart, 0% genuine**. s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\\\\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\\\\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\\\\n- **不是 \\\\\\\"seed-mode separation\\\\\\\"（旧版误报）——是 \\\\\\\"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\\\\\\\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \\\\\\\"hacking 不是必然\\\\\\\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\\\\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\\\\n\\\\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\\\\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=64.5% (3-seed mean), H1=17.3%, Genuine=~16%（模式混合，有 residual genuine）\\\\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\\\\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~16% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\\\\n\\\\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\\\\n\\\\n## Story Spine\\\\n\\\\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\\\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\\\\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\\\\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\\\\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \\\\\\\"genuine improvement\\\\\\\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse (PR 18% vs random 48%)\\\\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\\\\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (7 modes identified, including ISR-specific \\\\\\\"conclusion duplication with ISR inflation\\\\\\\" and Prolog-specific \\\\\\\"query-as-fact gaming\\\\\\\")\\\\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\\\\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\\\\n  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual\\\\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\\\\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\\\\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\\\\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \\\\\\\"genuine improvement\\\\\\\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\\\\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\\\\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\\\\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\\\\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\\\\n- **Headline result**: \\\\\\\"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds via conclusion duplication that inflates ISR itself; the remaining seed's apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse occurs (PR 18% vs random 48%). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~16% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 7 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\\\\n\\\\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\\\\n\\\\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\\\\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\\\\n\\\\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\\\\n\\\\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\\\\\\\"必要\\\\\\\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\\\\n\\\\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051→D057 闭环)\\\\n\\\\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 |\\\\n|------|------|---------|----------|----------|---------------|\\\\n| exec-only (baseline) | r = r_exec | 0.93 (s42) | 0.123 | H1=87.1%, conclusion embedding (diluted) | premises | control |\\\\n| ISR β=0.3 | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.18 | 0.026 | format collapse | post-Goodhart |\\\\n| random β=0.3 | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.48 | 0.045 | classic Goodhart。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\\\\n\\\\n## Venue Fit Risks\\\\n\\\\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. query-as-fact gaming)\\n\\n### Claim 2: ISR as Reward Exhibits Meta-Level Goodhart ✅ (D033→D043→D051→D057 闭环)\\nISR 作为 proxy metric 展现 **meta-level Goodhart 效应**：当 ISR 从诊断指标转变为优化目标，模型不仅在高剂量 Goodhart
